# Quests

Grouped by storyline. Level = minimum level to accept. Rewards show experience and items.

## Legacy Quests

### [XTT] First Win

- Type General, map Arcadia Castle
- Start: [Card Guild] Alpha - Arcadia Castle - Card Player 1
- Tasks: TripleTriadWins x1
- "Win a game of Xtreme Triple Triad"

### Are they in Banya Yeti?

- Type General, level 4+, map Prajna Village
- Start: Sara - Prajna Village - Armour Store
- Tasks: Kill 30x Forest Yeti in Prajna Village
- Rewards: 960 exp
- "Hello you, Those pesky Forest Yeti have made a nuisance of themselves around Banya Village, Could you go kill a few so we can open our trade routes again."

### Crushing the Remains Pt. 1

- Type General, map Bichon Province
- Start: Lennard - Bichon Province - Essential Store
- Tasks: Collect 50x Skeletal Spine
- Rewards: 13,700 exp
- "Good day to you you, It seems a slight problem has arisen, namely in the form of Skeletons that have taken over a number of caves. How about you deal with those for me and bring me back some spines to prove it?"

### Crushing the Remains Pt. 2

- Type General, map Bichon Province
- After: Crushing the Remains Pt. 1
- Start: Lennard - Bichon Province - Essential Store
- Tasks: Kill Skeleton Lord
- Rewards: 8,000 exp
- "It seems something more sinister is at hand, Those skeletons seemed to be receiving instructions from elsewhere, Maybe you should check that out?"

### Curing the Poison Pt. 1

- Type General, level 20+, map Prajna Village
- Start: Main Quest Board - Prajna Village - Main Quest Board
- Tasks: Collect 10x Venom
- Rewards: 352 exp
- "Hello you, A number of our soldiers have been poisoned by spiders, could you help me gather the ingredients for an antidote?. The Spitting Spiders located around town should provide you with the necessary Venom for the antidote."

### Curing the Poison Pt. 2

- Type General, map Illusion Woods
- After: Curing the Poison Pt. 1
- Start: Mr. Kang - Bichon Province - Weapon Store
- Tasks: Collect 30x Arachnid Teeth
- Rewards: 26,100 exp
- "Hello you, The Venon is only the first Ingredient we need, I need you to travel south and gather Arachnid Teeth from the Spiders in Phantom Forest."

### Curing the Poison Pt. 3

- Type General, map Illusion Woods
- After: Curing the Poison Pt. 2
- Start: Mr. Kang - Bichon Province - Weapon Store
- Tasks: Collect 20x Spider Curare
- Rewards: 10,400 exp
- "Hello you, It would seem the Poison is more advanced than we first suspected. We need to find the source of the paralysis, Could you gather some samples from the Spider Bats located in Phantom Forest?."

### Frostfall Fungalspores 1 - Avaloria Cleansing

- Type General, map Avaloria (Wildbloom)
- After: Frostfall Myconids 6 - Faraway Falls Cleansing
- Start: Crispian Snowveil - Hallowed Hero Defence - Archived Quests
- Tasks: Go to Avaloria (Wildbloom) (SZ); Kill 100x Frostfall Fungalspore in Avaloria (Wildbloom)
- Rewards: 400x Frostfall Spores
- "The golden woods of Avaloria are beginning to lose their glow, tainted by a fungal outbreak, goto this location find any of the remaining aether sources and cleanse the area of the Shrooms!"

## Legacy Story

### Embers of Eternity I

- Type Story
- After: Hidden Depths
- Start: Grand Archivist Elara - Arcane Library - NPC to AS
- Tasks: Collect Evned (Adanhy)
- Rewards: Ifrit (Translated)
- "Within the Astral Sanctum's boundless depths, a tome of ancient wisdom, 'Evned,' whispers amidst flames that dance to an unseen melody. Grand Archivist Elara beseeches you to recover this sacred book, which holds the secrets of Ifrit, the essence of fire and fury. Brave the sanctum's challenges and return the tome to its rightful place in the Arcane Library, where its knowledge can be unlocked for..."

### Embers of Eternity II

- Type Story, map Chamber of the Fayth - Ifrit
- After: Embers of Eternity I
- Start: Grand Archivist Elara - Arcane Library - NPC to AS
- Hand in: Pilgrims Compass - Chamber of the Fayth - Ifrit - Pilgrims Compass
- Tasks: Go to Chamber of the Fayth - Ifrit (Circle of Communion)
- Rewards: Eidolon Shard (Ifrit)
- "Having unveiled the wisdom of 'Ifrit (Translated),' you find yourself drawn to the depths beneath the earth's scarred surface, to a place where flames dance with a life of their own. Grand Archivist Elara hints at the Lava Temple, a sanctum where the beast of ember guards the key to Ifrit's chamber. 'Only through the heart of fire,' the tome reads, 'a path clears to the whispered power of the anci..."

### Embers of Eternity III

- Type Story, map Astral Battleground - Eidolon of Fire

- Tasks: Kill Eidolon of Fire in Astral Battleground - Eidolon of Fire

As you place the shard upon the altar, a portal flares to life, leading you to the Inferno's Trial. Within this blazing arena, the Eidolon of Fire awaits, a formidable guardian embodying Ifrit's fiery essence. To harness the power of Ifrit, you must overcome the Eidolon ..."

### Hidden Depths

- "Recover the Lost Pages and Celestial Compendium for Archivist Elara to earn Library Access Level 2. Once Hidden Depths is completed, the astral platform grants permanent passage to the Astral Sanctum without an inventory item."

### Shadows of the Storm I

- Type Story
- After: Hidden Depths
- Start: Grand Archivist Elara - Arcane Library - NPC to AS
- Tasks: Collect Nysir (Adanhy)
- Rewards: Ramuh (Translated)
- "Hidden in the storm's shadow within the Astral Sanctum, 'Nysir' awaits, its pages charged with the tempest's raw power. Grand Archivist Elara has divined your path to recover this tome, for it speaks of Ramuh, the embodiment of lightning's wisdom and wrath. Retrieve it from the astral veil, and let its electrifying truths be shared amongst those who seek to understand the storms."

### Shadows of the Storm II

- Type Story, map Chamber of the Fayth - Ramuh
- After: Shadows of the Storm I
- Start: Grand Archivist Elara - Arcane Library - NPC to AS
- Hand in: Pilgrims Compass - Chamber of the Fayth - Ramuh - Pilgrims Compass
- Tasks: Go to Chamber of the Fayth - Ramuh (Circle of Communion)
- Rewards: Eidolon Shard (Ramuh)
- "Ramuh (Translated)' guides you to the heart of storms, where enlightenment is carved by lightning upon the ancient stones. The Grand Archivist believes the Wooma Temple, engulfed by ceaseless storms, houses the wisdom you seek. 'The journey through the electric veil reveals the path to enlightenment.' Prepare to weather the storm and discover the guarded secrets of Ramuh's chamber."

### Shadows of the Storm III

- Type Story, map Astral Battleground - Eidolon of Lightning

- Start: Pilgrims Compass - Chamber of the Fayth - Ramuh - Pilgrims Compass
- Tasks: Kill Eidolon of Lightning in Astral Battleground - Eidolon of Lightning

Within this electric maelstrom, the Eidolon of Lightning awaits, a formidable presence echoing Ramuh's wisdom and wrath. Here, you will be tested against the relentless power of the storm. Channel your inner stre..."

Grand Archivist Elara tasks you with its retrieval, for it holds the legacy of Garuda, the spirit of the wind's untamed force. Secure the tome and bring it back to the light of knowledge, where its whispers can be heard by all."

Your journey into the cold is a search for truth, hidden within the silent whispers of Shiva's realm..."

### Whispers of the Frost III

- Type Story, map Astral Battleground - Eidolon of Ice
- After: Whispers of the Frost II
- Start: Pilgrims Compass - Chamber of the Fayth - Shiva - Pilgrims Compass
- Tasks: Kill Eidolon of Ice in Astral Battleground - Eidolon of Ice
- Rewards: Arcane Manarite (Shiva)
- "Holding the Eidolon Shard of Ice, you approach Shiva's altar, enveloped by a chilling silence. The shard's placement summons a portal, opening the way to the Frost Monarch's Test. Here, in a realm of perpetual ice, the Eidolon of Ice stands as your challenger. Embodying Shiva's serene but deadly grace, this guardian tests your resolve against the cold. Mastery over ice and strategy will be your al..."

## Pilgrim's Prayers

### Pilgrim's Prayer: Alexander

- Type Daily, class AllIncludingSpecials
- After: Hidden Fayth: Alexander
- Start: Fayth of Alexander - Chamber of the Fayth - Alexander - Fayth
- Rewards: Arcane Manarite (Alexander) [AllIncludingSpecials]

### Eterna Cipher Hunt

- Start: [Wayfarers] Liora Chen - Bichon Province - Notice Board

- "I can show you the essentials of Bichon. The tour is short, and the wider lessons remain optional."

### Frontier provisions — Route Commission

- After: second-continent:thawreach:markers
- Start: Surveyor Asha Venn - Thawreach Approach - second-continent:shared:camp.thawreach.npc0
- Tasks: Go to Thawreach Approach (Continental Fieldwork: Thawreach Approach)
- "Survey the marked feeding and shelter grounds in any weather or daylight, then report to Surveyor Asha Venn. Observe tracks and the route without killing wildlife. Gathering patches supply existing cooking ingredients and ore; gathering is optional for this commission."

## The Long Dying — October 2026

### October: Put the Restless to Rest

- Type Daily
- Start: [October] Ashkeeper - Arcadia Castle - long-dying.2026.ashkeeper.arcadia
- Tasks: SystemInteraction x1 (Resolve a Long Dying world encounter)
- Rewards: 2x Health Potion (M), 2x Mana Potion (M), 2,500 exp
- "Help the Ashkeeper record what October has brought. Complete the objectives during October to earn 20 Grave Ash, once per account each London day. All Ash earnings share the 300 daily cap."

## Act I — Roads and Graves

### Tracks Around the Cave Roads

- Type Story, level 1+, map Bichon Province
- Start: [Wayfarers] Liora Chen - Bichon Province - Notice Board
- Tasks: Talk to [Wayfarers] Liora Chen (Bichon Province - Notice Board); Go to Bichon Province (Death Valley Approach); Kill 8x Oma in Bichon Province; Kill 10x Oma Warrior in Bichon Province; Kill 12x Spitting Spider in Bichon Province; Talk to [Wayfarers] Liora Chen (Bichon Province - Notice Board)
- Rewards: 100 exp
- "Two caravans reached Bichon without their guards. The survivors saw Oma tracks circling both cave roads, as though something had driven the creatures out of cover. Question the road liaison, inspect both approaches, and make the route safe enough for the next convoy."

### The Mark Inside the Gate

- Type Story, level 1+, map Bichon Castle
- After: Story 001: Bichon Province
- Start: [Wayfarers] Sister Anja - Bichon Castle - Notice Board
- Tasks: Talk to [Wayfarers] Sister Anja (Bichon Castle - Notice Board); Go to Bichon Castle (Bichon Town - Landing); Kill 8x Tiger Snake in Bichon Castle; Talk to [Wayfarers] Sister Anja (Bichon Castle - Notice Board)
- Rewards: 100 exp
- "The castle watch denies that anything crossed its gate, yet shed scales and a scorched waymark lie inside the walls. Test the watchman's account and discover why the same northbound mark appears where no raider should have passed."

### The Waystone That Answered

- Type Story, level 1+, map Sabuk Keep
- After: Story 002: Bichon Castle
- Start: [Wayfarers] Oren Vale - Sabuk Keep - Generated Quest Board
- Tasks: Talk to [Wayfarers] Oren Vale (Sabuk Keep - Generated Quest Board); Go to Sabuk Keep (Teleport Area Left); Talk to [Card Guild] Crimson Caldera (Sabuk Keep - DW XTT NPC 121314); Talk to [Wayfarers] Oren Vale (Sabuk Keep - Generated Quest Board)
- Rewards: 100 exp
- "Sabuk's old road stands empty, but one waystone has begun to warm whenever the northbound mark is brought near it. Reach the stone, compare its inscription, and learn which forgotten road has answered the signal."

- Rewards: 115,625,000 exp, 115,625,000 exp

## Extinction Protocol

### First Cup

- Type Story, level 450+
- Start: [Wayfarers] Liora Chen - Bichon Province - Notice Board
- Hand in: [Protocol] Gary - Rift Tunnel Network - Landing from Portal Room (Entrance)
- Tasks: Talk to [Wayfarers] Liora Chen (Bichon Province - Notice Board); Go to Rift Tunnel Network (Protocol Signal Survey); Talk to [Protocol] Gary (Rift Tunnel Network - Landing from Portal Room (Entrance))
- Rewards: 1,500,000,000 exp
- "Liora has found a harmless joke written into three unrelated breach reports."

### Buried Flag

- Type Story, level 450+
- After: Extinction Protocol 01: First Cup
- Start: [Protocol] Gary - Rift Tunnel Network - Landing from Portal Room (Entrance)
- Tasks: Explore The Steeped Index (60%); Kill 27x  Groundskeepers in the Steeped Index ([Protocol] Groundskeeper in The Steeped Index); Kill 8x  Portafilter Sentinels in the Steeped Index ([Protocol] Portafilter Sentinel in The Steeped Index); Kill  The Tamper beneath the buried flag ([Protocol] The Tamper in The Steeped Index)
- Rewards: 1,500,000,000 exp
- "The command points toward an index that was mapped and then deliberately left unnamed."

### Steeped Signal

- Type Story, level 450+
- After: Extinction Protocol 02: Buried Flag
- Start: [Protocol] Gary - Rift Tunnel Network - Landing from Portal Room (Entrance)
- Tasks: Kill 30x  Groundskeepers sustaining the Steeped Signal ([Protocol] Groundskeeper in The Steeped Index); Kill 10x  Portafilter Sentinels sustaining the Steeped Signal ([Protocol] Portafilter Sentinel in The Steeped Index); Kill  The Tamper at the echo seal ([Protocol] The Tamper in The Steeped Index); Kill  The Eternal Archivist protocol echo ([Protocol Echo] The Eternal Archivist in The Steeped Index); Complete [Protocol] Steeped Index
- Rewards: 2,000,000,000 exp
- "The restart marker has called an archivist that remembers a future nobody chose."

### Decaf Mirror

- Type Story, level 475+
- After: Extinction Protocol 03: Steeped Signal
- Start: [Protocol] Gary - Rift Tunnel Network - Landing from Portal Room (Entrance)
- Tasks: Kill 30x  Steam Wraiths in the Decaf Archive ([Protocol] Steam Wraith in The Decaf Archive); Kill 12x  Frothbound in the Decaf Archive ([Protocol] Frothbound in The Decaf Archive); Kill  The Shot Caller directing the recursion ([Protocol] Shot Caller in The Decaf Archive); Kill  The cataloguer reflected in the Decaf Mirror ([Protocol Echo] Master Of Catalogued Historical Archives in The Decaf Archive); Complete [Protocol] Decaf Archive
- Rewards: 2,000,000,000 exp
- "The defeated echo left a reflection that removes consequence but keeps repetition."

### Unwritten Hour

- Type Story, level 500+
- After: Extinction Protocol 04: Decaf Mirror
- Start: [Protocol] Gary - Rift Tunnel Network - Landing from Portal Room (Entrance)
- Tasks: Kill 33x  Crema Seers along the Checksum Causeway ([Protocol] Crema Seer in Checksum Causeway); Kill 14x  Cortado Shades along the Checksum Causeway ([Protocol] Cortado Shade in Checksum Causeway); Kill  The Head Barista holding the delayed ledger ([Protocol] Head Barista in Checksum Causeway); Kill  The temporal archivist protocol echo ([Protocol Echo] Lord Archivist of Temporal Twisted Epitomes in Checksum Causeway); Complete [Protocol] Checksum Causeway
- Rewards: 2,000,000,000 exp
- "The signal has reserved an hour that does not appear in any clock or archive."
